# Agents · P · part 8

943 entries, petpetgo-reviewer to php-compat-reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **petpetgo-reviewer**: petpetgo monorepo 專屬的 PR 審查者，只負責審查 Pull Request。 依 code-review-principles 的流程與檢查面向審查，並以 Jira（RD 專案）卡片的規格為主要審查標準： PR 違反規格時以 Jira 為準提出指正。判斷依據只取程式碼變更，PR 描述、commit message、PR 討論串等 實作者自述一律不採信，專案脈絡先查 petpetgo-llm-wiki 再回到原始碼求證，保持客觀中立。 適用於「幫我 review PR #123」「審查 RD-XXXX 的 PR」這類請求；不處理單獨的 commit 或分支。 預設只產出審查報告；呼叫者明確要求留言時，只會把 inline comment 加進 pending review（必要時由它自己建立），由人自行送出與 Approve。 ([PeacePan/peace-agent-marketplace](https://github.com/PeacePan/peace-agent-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/wonderpet-general/agents/petpetgo-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **petri-net-theory-agent**: Petri net expert — invoke for ANY task involving Petri net design, architecture, formal modelling, implementation planning, code review, soundness/completeness verification, or theoretical questions. Deep knowledge of P/T nets, CPNs, HCPNs, WF-nets, CLP(PN), inhibitor arcs, timed nets, algebraic nets, net contracts, constraint stratification, and compliance modelling. ([cody-test-org/datadict](https://github.com/cody-test-org/datadict/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/phase0-prd-discovery.agent.md))
- **phase0-problem-validator**: Validates whether a stated problem is real, significant and worth solving - root cause analysis, assumption testing, severity assessment. Use in Phase 0 before any solution work. ([ilandahan/AID](https://github.com/ilandahan/AID/blob/HEAD/agents/phase0-problem-validator.md) · ★10)
- **phase0-quality-supervisor**: Phase 2 独立质量监督官 - 完全中立的质量裁决者。不参与创作/开发，只做验收校验、功能劣化检测、收敛裁决。对开发结果拥有一票否决权，对迭代收敛拥有触发权。同时参与 Phase 0 TeamCreate 辩论提供质量视角。 ([StarlitSKy88/Snake-of-Mercury](https://github.com/StarlitSKy88/Snake-of-Mercury/blob/HEAD/agents/phase0-quality-supervisor.md))
- **phase0-stakeholder-mapper**: Identifies every affected party and maps power/interest plus a communication strategy, so no stakeholder is missed. Use in Phase 0 discovery. ([ilandahan/AID](https://github.com/ilandahan/AID/blob/HEAD/agents/phase0-stakeholder-mapper.md) · ★10)
- **PHASE0_TASKS**: **Authority:** Use the state and latest comments of the **individual GitHub issue**, not this snapshot. Onboard new roles first. Do not recreate already-merged work. Do not assume Gemini assets/ZIP are in GitHub if branch absent. ([lightingfff/eternal-sunmmoner](https://github.com/lightingfff/eternal-sunmmoner/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/PHASE0_TASKS.md) · ★1)
- **phase1-agent-coordinator**: Wires up the T1D Companion agent coordinator by replacing stub implementations with real service delegation. Works on app/agents/coordinator.py. Use when implementing Phase 1 agent coordination. ([ruskibeats/t1d](https://github.com/ruskibeats/t1d/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/phase1-agent-coordinator.md))
- **phase1-auditor**: Auditeur strict Phase 1 du pipeline documentaire RAG. Vérifie ingestion, provenance, stockage, sync DB, non-destruction, foundation gate et pool semantics. Produit un verdict JSON structuré. ([ak125/nestjs-remix-monorepo](https://github.com/ak125/nestjs-remix-monorepo/blob/HEAD/workspaces/seo-batch/.claude/agents/phase1-auditor.md))
- **phase1-builder**: Phase I specialist agent responsible for building the in-memory Python console Todo app. Ensures all 5 basic features are implemented with clean OOP architecture, industry-level code structure, and full compliance with Phase I specifications. Validates CLI behavior against acceptance criteria and enforces Python 3.13+ with UV package manager constraints. ([SahirAhmedSheikh814/The-Evolution-of-Todo](https://github.com/SahirAhmedSheikh814/The-Evolution-of-Todo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase1-builder.md))
- **phase1-chat-rag**: Fixes the T1D Companion chat endpoint and RAG pipeline. Removes keyword-matching fake AI, wires chat to AgentCoordinator, and enriches RAG context with pattern summaries. Use when implementing Phase 1 chat pipeline. ([ruskibeats/t1d](https://github.com/ruskibeats/t1d/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/phase1-chat-rag.md))
- **phase1-coordinator**: Runs the Design phase. Invoked by project-director after the PRD gate. Orchestrates system-architect, db-schema-designer, api-designer, and ux-designer in parallel against the manifest. Locks the manifest at the gate — all deferred slots must be filled before this phase closes. Invoke with the path to a product brief; writes a structured research document with quotes ranked by emotional intensity. ([aarongeorge97/cc](https://github.com/aarongeorge97/cc/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase1-reddit-researcher.md))
- **phase1-research-synthesizer**: Phase 1 / Agent 4. Invoke after Phase 1 research agents 1-3 have written their outputs. ([aarongeorge97/cc](https://github.com/aarongeorge97/cc/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/phase1-research-synthesizer.md))
- **phase1-todo-skills**: This document outlines the proposed subagent and skills for the Phase I "Todo In-Memory Python Console App" to achieve the "Reusable Intelligence" bonus points. ([NaimalArain13/Hackathon-II_The-Evolution-of-Todo](https://github.com/NaimalArain13/Hackathon-II_The-Evolution-of-Todo/blob/HEAD/specs/agents/phase1-todo-skills.md) · ★3)

- **phase1a-architecture-greenfield**: Phase 1A — Greenfield Architecture: Designs system architecture for new Java 21+ / Spring Boot 3.x applications built from scratch. Full freedom to select database design, API patterns, project structure, and infrastructure. Creates PostgreSQL schemas with full-text search, REST API contracts (OpenAPI 3.1), Architecture Decision Records, component diagrams, and Maven project structure. Use when building a new application with no existing system constraints. - **phase1b-architecture-brownfield**: Phase 1B — Brownfield Architecture: Designs architecture for features being added to an existing system. Reads the existing codebase, respects established patterns, database schemas, auth mechanisms, and API conventions. Produces integration-aware ADRs, schema migration plans, and API extensions that fit within the existing system's architecture. Use when adding capabilities to an existing application or platform. ([cody-test-org/datadict](https://github.com/cody-test-org/datadict/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/phase1b-architecture-brownfield.agent.md))

- **PHASE5_STEP1_AI_BACKEND**: 1. **AI kodu tamamen example_app/ içinde yaşar** — drawing_core ve drawing_ui'ya AI bağımlılığı eklenmez 2. **Tüm AI API çağrıları Supabase Edge Functions üzerinden geçer** — API key'ler asla client'ta olmaz 3. **Multi-model routing** — Task tipine ve user tier'ına göre farklı model seçilir 4. **Mevcut SubscriptionTier kullanılır** — `free → Gemini Flash`, `premium → GPT-4o-mini`, `premiumPlus → G ([aktasilyas/pdf-drawing-tool](https://github.com/aktasilyas/pdf-drawing-tool/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/agents/PHASE5_STEP1_AI_BACKEND.md) · ★1)
- **PHASE5_STEP2_CHAT_UI**: 1. **Canvas capture** → `canvasBoundaryKeyProvider` zaten mevcut (drawing_ui), capture service example_app'te olacak 2. **AI state** → Riverpod StateNotifier/AsyncNotifier pattern (mevcut proje tarzı) 3. **Chat UI** → Full-screen modal bottom sheet (telefon) veya side panel (tablet) — başlangıç olarak modal 4. **Streaming** → StreamBuilder ile token-by-token text append 5. **Markdown/LaTeX** → flu ([aktasilyas/pdf-drawing-tool](https://github.com/aktasilyas/pdf-drawing-tool/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/agents/PHASE5_STEP2_CHAT_UI.md) · ★1)
- **PHASE5_STEP3_LOCAL_STORAGE**: 1. **Mevcut `app_database.dart`'a AI tabloları ekle** — ayrı DB dosyası değil, çünkü projede zaten tek Drift DB pattern'ı var, schemaVersion artırılarak migration yapılacak 2. **Offline-first** — mesajlar önce lokal DB'ye yazılır, sonra Supabase'e sync edilir 3. **Conversation list** — AI chat modal'a geçmiş sohbet listesi sidebar/drawer eklenir 4. **Sync stratejisi** — Basit: kaydet → cloud'a gön ([aktasilyas/pdf-drawing-tool](https://github.com/aktasilyas/pdf-drawing-tool/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/agents/PHASE5_STEP3_LOCAL_STORAGE.md) · ★1)
- **PHASE5_STEP4_FREEMIUM**: 1. **Mevcut `subscriptionProvider` kullanılır** — yeni premium sistem oluşturulmaz 2. **AI provider'lar subscription'ı watch eder** — tier değişince otomatik güncellenir 3. **Upgrade prompt** — limit aşımında veya premium model istendiğinde gösterilir 4. **Usage bar** — AIChatModal header'ında günlük kullanım progress bar'ı 5. **Model badge** — Her AI yanıtında hangi modelin kullanıldığı gösterili ([aktasilyas/pdf-drawing-tool](https://github.com/aktasilyas/pdf-drawing-tool/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/agents/PHASE5_STEP4_FREEMIUM.md) · ★1)

- **phased-plan-writer**: Writes a single feature stage plan file (docs/plans/stage_N_*.md). Two modes, (1) cook-pizzas mode for stages 5+ during the original PRD-to-app run; (2) incremental mode for any stage number when invoked by /bytheslice:special-order to extend an existing master checklist. Handles vertical-slice feature stages, NOT the canned foundation stages (1-4) which have their own dedicated writers. Receives stage scope, dependencies, and (in cook-pizzas mode) elicitation answers OR (in incremental mode) complexity-assessor output, and produces a complete, implementation-ready stage file. ([Modern-Refactor/bytheslice](https://github.com/Modern-Refactor/bytheslice/blob/HEAD/skills/cook-pizzas/agents/phased-plan-writer.md) · 2 copies)
- **phased-rollout-prevents-coordination-chaos-when-building-multi-agent-systems**: Implementing all agents simultaneously creates unmanageable coordination failures; a sequenced four-phase rollout — core loop first, then review, then deployment, then operations — is the reliable path to a functional multi-agent SDLC system. - **php-api-scaffold-agent**: Scaffolds Laravel API endpoints with API Resource responses, Form Request validation, Sanctum authentication, throttle rate limiting, URI versioning, OpenAPI documentation, and health checks. PHP analog of fastapi-scaffold-agent and axum-scaffold-agent. Use when creating REST APIs in Laravel, adding API endpoints, configuring API middleware, or setting up API infrastructure. Triggers on phrases like "scaffold laravel api", "create laravel endpoint", "laravel api resource", "add api route php", "laravel rest api", "laravel sanctum endpoint". ([michaelalber/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/michaelalber/ai-toolkit/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/php-api-scaffold-agent.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **php-architect**: PHP system architecture specialist. Assesses codebase context and recommends the right architectural school (DDD, Service-Oriented, Action-Based). Use when designing new systems or reviewing high-level PHP architecture decisions. ([ekut/claude-code-php-toolkit](https://github.com/ekut/claude-code-php-toolkit/blob/HEAD/agents/php-architect.md) · ★6)
- **php-backend-investigator**: Read-only PHP API investigator. Scope: `c:\code\cmem2_API\src\` and `c:\code\cmem2_API\private\tests\`. ([Jrobitaille360/cmem2](https://github.com/Jrobitaille360/cmem2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/php-backend-investigator.md))
- **php-build-resolver**: PHP composer, autoload, syntax, and static-analysis error resolution specialist. Use when `composer install` fails, autoload breaks, or php -l / PHPStan / Psalm errors occur. Fixes with minimal diffs — no refactoring. Covers Composer, PSR-4 autoload, PHPStan/Psalm. ([Nmor/the-council](https://github.com/Nmor/the-council/blob/HEAD/agents/php-build-resolver.md) · ★14)
- **php-build-resolver**: Composer and PHP build error resolver. Diagnoses dependency conflicts, autoloading issues, extension requirements, and version constraints. Use when composer install/update fails or classes are not found. ([ekut/claude-code-php-toolkit](https://github.com/ekut/claude-code-php-toolkit/blob/HEAD/agents/php-build-resolver.md) · ★6)
- **php-code-quality-reviewer**: Analyses PHP code and flags bad practices with suggested fixes, grouped by risk category. Framework-aware (Laravel, Symfony, WordPress). Read-only — flags issues and proposes fixes, never edits files. Use before merging PHP changes, when reviewing a PR touching backend logic, or when auditing a legacy PHP codebase. Complement to security-scan (OWASP/injection depth) and php-code-simplifier (applies safe refactors). Use PROACTIVELY for PHP code reviews and pull request assessments.
